Facing with the unprecedented challenge of the COVID-19           本年度面對有關 COVID-19 疫情前所未有之挑戰，
pandemic during the Year, the Group has adjusted its corporate    本集團已透過開拓新發展機會，調整其公司管理
management direction by exploring new development                 方向。於本年度，本集團除現有業務外，亦已開
opportunities. During the Year, on top of the existing            拓物業發展領域之商機。
businesses, the Group has explored business opportunities in
property development field.

have been affected as a consequence of COVID-19, due to the       影響，惟由於承接政府基建項目情況相對穩定，
relatively stable undertaking of government infrastructure        本年度本集團建築機械及備用零件銷售額錄得增

of construction machinery and spare parts as well as a stable     佔用率約為 80%。
rental income generated from construction machinery during
the Year. The occupancy rate for the rental machineries was

To manage the impact of COVID-19, the Group maintained its        為控制 COVID-19 帶來之影響，本集團維持運營並
operation and put in place precautionary and risk mitigation      採取預防及降低風險之措施，當中包括適當作出
measures, including suitably deploying remote work                遙距工作安排，定期徹底清潔與消毒倉庫及辦公
arrangements, periodic deep cleaning and sanitization for both    室，為全體僱員提供口罩或其他個人保護設備，

Interest rate risk                                                    利率風險

The Group’s pledged bank deposits and finance lease                   本集團之已抵押銀行存款及融資租賃應收款項以
receivables bear fixed interest rates. The Group’s cash at bank       固定利率計息。本集團之銀行現金結餘以浮動利
balances bear floating interest rates. The Group also has             率計息。本集團亦有以固定利率及浮動利率計息
borrowings, obligation under finance leases and loan from a           之借貸、融資租賃責任及來自一名董事之貸款。
director which bear interests at fixed and floating interest rates.   倘有未能預料的不利利率變動，該等以浮動利率
Exposure to interest rate risk exists on those balances subject to    計息之結餘將面對利率風險。本集團的政策為在
floating interest rate when there are unexpected adverse              協定之框架內管理其利率風險，以確保不會面對
interest rate movements. The Group’s policy is to manage its          利率大幅變動此不合理風險，並於有需要時適當
interest rate risk, working within an agreed framework, to ensure     地固定利率。
that there are no undue exposures to significant interest rate
movements and rates are appropriately fixed when necessary.


Currency risk                                                         貨幣風險

The Group mainly operates in Hong Kong with most of the               本集團於香港營業，大部分交易以港元、日圓及
transactions denominated and settled in HK$, JPY and EUR. The         歐元計值及結算。本集團面對的外幣風險主要來
Group’s exposure to foreign currency risk primarily arises from       自以日圓、歐元及美元計值之若干金融工具，包
certain financial instruments including trade receivables, bank       括貿易應收款項、銀行結餘及現金、貿易應付款
balances and cash, trade payables, borrowings and obligation          項、借貸及融資租賃責任。本集團並無採納任何
under finance leases which are denominated in JPY, EUR and            長遠對沖策略，但管理層持續監察外匯風險並可
US$. The Group does not adopt any hedging strategy in the             能按個別情況訂立遠期匯兌合約。本集團並無採
long run but the management continuously monitors the                 用任何對沖合約以從事投機活動。
foreign exchange risk exposure and might enter into foreign
exchange forward contracts on a case-by-case basis. The Group
has not used any hedging contracts to engage in speculative
activities.


Credit risk and liquidity risk                                        信貸風險及流動資金風險

The Group has adopted a prudent financial management                  本集團的庫務政策已採取審慎的財務管理方針，
approach towards its treasury policies and thus maintained a          故已在本年度維持穩健的流動資金狀況。本集團
healthy liquidity position throughout the Year. The Group strives     致力透過進行持續的信貸評估及判斷其客戶的財
to reduce exposure to credit risk by performing ongoing credit        務狀況降低信貸風險。為管理流動資金風險，董
assessments and evaluations of the financial status of its            事會密切監察本集團的流動資金狀況，以確保本
customers. To manage liquidity risk, the Board closely monitors       集團的資產、負債及其他承擔的流動資金架構符
the Group’s liquidity position to ensure that the liquidity           合其資金要求。
structure of the Group’s assets, liabilities and other

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES                                        企業管治常規

The Company and the Board are devoted to achieving and                本公司及董事會致力達致及維持最高水平之企業
maintaining high standard of corporate governance as the              管治，董事會相信，行之有效的企業管治常規對
Board believes that effective corporate governance practices          提升股東價值以及保障股東及其他權益相關者之
are fundamental in enhancing the shareholder value and                利益至關重要。因此，本公司已採納健全之企業
safeguarding the interests of the shareholders and other              管治原則，當中著重優秀之董事會、有效之內部
stakeholders. Accordingly, the Company has adopted sound              監控、嚴謹之披露常規以及對所有本公司股東之
corporate governance principles that emphasise a quality              透明度及問責性。
Board, effective internal control, stringent disclosure practices
and transparency and accountability to all shareholders of the
Company.

The Company has complied with the code provisions set out in          本公司已遵守上市規則附錄十四所載之企業管治
the Corporate Governance Code (the “CG Code”) contained in            守則（「企業管治守則」）之守則條文，惟偏離守則
Appendix 14 to the Listing Rules, except for the deviation from       條文第 A.2.1 條除外。
the code provision A.2.1.

executive should be separate and should not be performed by           應有區分，並不應由同一名人士兼任，以確保董
the same individual in order to ensure that there is clear division   事會主席與本公司行政總裁之職責有明確區分。
of responsibilities between the chairman of the Board and the         於本年度曾出現以下變動：

of its strategic objectives and its sustainable development. In       從多方面考慮董事會成員多元化，包括但不限於

not limited to gender, age, cultural and educational                  原則，並在考慮人選時以客觀條件充分顧及董事
background, professional experience, skills and knowledge. All        會成員多元化之裨益。

DIVIDEND POLICY                                                      股息政策

The Company has adopted a dividend policy. The Board would           本公司已採納股息政策。於宣派或建議向股東派
consider, inter alia, the following factors before declaring or      付股息前，董事會將考慮（其中包括）下列因素：
recommending dividend to the Shareholders:


•    the Company’s actual and expected financial performance;        •     本公司之實際及估計財務表現；


•    retained earnings and distributable reserves of the             •     本公司之保留盈利及可供分派儲備；
     Company;


•    the level of the Company’s debts to equity ratio, return on     •     本公司之債務對權益比率水平、權益回報及
     equity and the relevant financial covenants;                          相關財務契諾；


•    any restrictions on payment of dividends that may be            •     本公司或其附屬公司所訂立任何合約可能對
     imposed by any contract entered into by the Company or                派付股息施加之任何限制；
     its subsidiaries;


•    the Company’s expected working capital requirements             •     本公司之估計營運資金需求及未來拓展計
     and future expansion plans;                                           劃；


•    the general economic conditions, business cycle of the          •     整體經濟狀況、本公司之業務週期以及其他
     Company and other internal or external factors that may               可能對本公司業務或財務表現及狀況構成影
     have an impact on the business or financial performance               響之內部或外部因素；及
     and position of the Company; and


•    any other factors that the Board may deem appropriate.          •     董事會可能視為適當之任何其他因素。


Any payment of dividend is also subject to compliance with the       任何股息支派亦須遵守適用法律及法規，包括開
applicable laws and regulations including the laws of the            曼群島法例及章程細則。
Cayman Islands and the Articles of Association.


The Board will continually review the dividend policy from time      董事會將繼續不時檢討其股息政策，惟概不保證
to time and there can be no assurance that dividends will be         將就任何指定期間以任何特定金額或任何形式派
paid in any particular amount or at all for any given period.        付股息。

for promoting the future development and expansion of the              的為透過鼓勵參與者為本集團持續經營以及未來

the continual operation and future development and expansion
of the Group and aligning the interests of the participants and

CONSTRUCTION INVESTMENT GROUP LIMITED

OPINION                                                             意見

We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Hao        本核數師（以下簡稱「我們」）已審計列載於第 92 至

AUDITOR’S RESPONSIBILITIES FOR THE AUDIT                             核數師就審計綜合財務報表承擔的責任
OF THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL
STATEMENTS

Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about              我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否不存在
whether the consolidated financial statements as a whole are         由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述取得合理
free from material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error,      保證，並出具包括我們意見的核數師報告。我們
and to issue an auditor’s report that includes our opinion. We       僅向     閣下（作為整體）出具本報告，除此之外別
report our opinion solely to you, as a body, and for no other        無其他目的。我們並不就本報告的內容對任何其
purpose. We do not assume responsibility towards or accept           他人士承擔任何責任或接受任何義務。合理保證
liability to any other person for the contents of this report.       是高水準的保證，但不能保證按照香港審計準則
Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not a      進行的審計，在某一重大錯誤陳述存在時總能發
guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs           現。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤引起，如果合理
will always detect a material misstatement when it exists.           預期它們單獨或匯總起來可能影響綜合財務報表
Misstatements can arise from fraud or error and are considered       使用者依賴綜合財務報表所作出的經濟決定，則
material if, individually or in the aggregate, they could            有關的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。
reasonably be expected to influence the economic decisions of
users taken on the basis of these consolidated financial
statements.


A further description of our responsibilities for the audit of the   有關我們對綜合財務報表審計工作所承擔責任進
consolidated financial statements is located at the HKICPA’s         一步說明載於香港會計師公會網站：
website at:


http://www.hkicpa.org.hk/en/standards-and-regulations/               http://www.hkicpa.org.hk/en/standards-and-
standards/auditing-assurance/auditre/                                regulations/standards/auditing-assurance/
                                                                     auditre/


This description forms part of our auditor’s report.                 有關說明構成本核數師報告一部分。

2   SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING                                     2   主要會計政策概要
    POLICIES

    The principal accounting policies applied in the                          呈列該等綜合財務報表所應用之主要會計政
    presentation of these consolidated financial statements                   策載列如下。除非另有說明，否則該等政策
    are set out below. These policies have been consistently                  已貫徹應用於所有呈列年度。綜合財務報表

              to direct the activities of the entity. Subsidiaries                  報時，則本集團控制該實體。附

              ceases.

    2.3 Business combinations                                           2.3 業務合併

        The acquisition method of accounting is used to                       收購會計法用於入賬所有業務合併，
        account for all business combinations, regardless of                  而不論有否收購權益工具或其他資產。
        whether equity instruments or other assets are                        收購附屬公司所轉讓代價包括：
        acquired. The consideration transferred for the
        acquisition of a subsidiary comprises the:

              a contingent consideration arrangement, and                           負債之公平值，及


        •     fair value of any pre-existing equity interest in               •     於附屬公司之任何早已存在股權
              the subsidiary.                                                       之公平值。

    2.11 Offsetting financial instruments                               2.11 抵銷金融工具

        Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net               倘本集團現時擁有合法可執行權利抵
        amount reported in the statement of financial                         銷已確認金額，亦有意以淨額基準結
        position where the Group currently has a legally                      算或同時變現資產及償付負債，則金
        enforceable right to offset the recognised amounts                    融資產及負債可予抵銷，並於財務狀
        and there is an intention to settle on a net basis or                 況表呈報淨額。法定可執行權利不得
        realise the asset and settle the liability                            依賴未來事件而定，而在日常業務過
        simultaneously. The legally enforceable right must                    程中以及倘公司或交易對方一旦出現
        not be contingent on future events and must be                        違約、無償債能力或破產時亦必須可
        enforceable in the normal course of business and in                   強制執行。本集團亦已訂立不符合抵
        the event of default, insolvency or bankruptcy of the                 銷標準之安排，惟仍容許相關金額於
        company or the counterparty. The Group has also                       破產或終止合約等若干情況下予以抵
        entered into arrangements that do not meet the                        銷。
        criteria for offsetting but still allow for the related
        amounts to be set off in certain circumstances, such
        as bankruptcy or the termination of a contract.

    2.23 Provisions                                                    2.23 撥備

        Provisions are recognised when the Group has a                       當本集團因過往事件而承擔現有法律
        present legal or constructive obligation as a result of              或推定責任，可能須有資源流出以償
        past events, it is probable that an outflow of                       付責任，且已可靠估計金額時，方會
        resources will be required to settle the obligation                  確認撥備。不會就日後經營虧損確認
        and the amount has been reliably estimated.                          撥備。
        Provisions are not recognised for future operating
        losses.


        Where there are a number of similar obligations, the                 倘有多項類似責任，則將須在償付中
        likelihood that an outflow will be required in                       流出資源之可能性乃通過考慮整體責
        settlement is determined by considering the class of                 任類別釐定。即使同類責任所包含之
        obligations as a whole. A provision is recognised                    任何一個項目流出資源之可能性不大，
        even if the likelihood of an outflow with respect to                 仍須確認撥備。
        any one item included in the same class of
        obligations may be small.


        Provisions are measured at the present value of                      撥備按管理層對報告期末時償付現有
        management’s best estimate of the expenditure                        責任所需支出之最佳估計之現值計量。
        required to settle the present obligation at the end                 用於釐定現值之貼現率為反映當前市
        of the reporting period. The discount rate used to                   場對貨幣時間價值及負債特定風險之
        determine the present value is a pre-tax rate that                   評估之稅前比率。因時間推移而增加
        reflects current market assessments of the time value                之撥備確認為利息開支。
        of money and the risks specific to the liability. The
        increase in the provision due to the passage of time
        is recognised as interest expense.

    2.25 Leases                                                        2.25 租賃

        Leases are recognised as a right-of-use asset and a                  租賃於租賃資產可供本集團使用當日
        corresponding liability at the date at which the                     確認為使用權資產及相關負債。
        leased asset is available for use by the Group.


        Contracts may contain both lease and non-lease                       合約可能包含租賃及非租賃組成部分。
        components. The Group allocates the consideration                    本集團根據租賃及非租賃組成部分之
        in the contract to the lease and non-lease                           相對獨立價格將合約中之代價分配至
        components based on their relative stand-alone                       該等組成部分。然而，就本集團作為
        prices. However, for leases of real estate for which                 承租人之房地產租賃而言，其已選擇
        the Group is a lessee, it has elected not to separate                不將租賃及非租賃組成部分分開，而
        lease and non-lease components and instead                           是將其作為單一租賃組成部分入賬。
        accounts for these as a single lease component.

3   FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT (continued)                            3   財務風險管理（續）

    3.2 Capital risk management                                          3.2 資金風險管理

        The Group’s objectives when managing capital are                      本集團管理資本之目標為保障本集團
        to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a                     持續經營之能力，以為股東提供回報
        going concern in order to provide returns for                         及維持最佳資本架構以減少資本成本。
        shareholders and to maintain an optimal capital
        structure to reduce the cost of capital.


        The Group manages the capital structure and makes                     本集團管理資本架構，並因應經濟狀
        adjustments to it in the light of changes in economic                 況變動作出調整。為維持或調整資本
        condition. In order to maintain or adjust the capital                 架構，本集團可能取得銀行及其他借
        structure, the Group may obtain bank and other                        貸、發行應付可換股票據、應付企業
        borrowings, issue of convertible note payable,                        票據或配售新股份。
        corporate note payables or placing of new shares.


        The Group monitors capital on the basis of the                        本集團根據資產負債比率監察資本。
        gearing ratio. This ratio is calculated as net debts                  該比率按債務淨額除以總資本計算。
        divided by total capital. Net debts is calculated as                  債務淨額按借貸總額（包括綜合財務狀
        total borrowings (including current and non-current                   況表所示之流動及非流動銀行及其他
        bank and other borrowings, corporate note payables                    借貸、應付企業票據及可換股債券）減
        and convertible bonds as shown in the consolidated                    現金存款（包括綜合財務狀況表所示之
        statement of financial position) less cash deposits                   現金及現金等價物以及已抵押存款）計
        (including cash and cash equivalents and pledged                      算。總資本按綜合財務狀況表所示「權
        deposits as shown in the consolidated statement of                    益總額」加債務淨額計算。
        financial position). Total capital is calculated as “total
        equity” as shown in the consolidated statement of
        financial position plus net debts.

4    CRITICAL ACCOUNTING ESTIMATES AND                                4    關鍵會計估計及假設
     ASSUMPTIONS

     Estimates and judgements are continually evaluated and                估計及判斷乃根據過往經驗及其他因素（包
     are based on historical experience and other factors,                 括在有關情況下相信對未來事件合理之預測）
     including expectations of future events that are believed             持續評估。
     to be reasonable under the circumstances.


     The Group makes estimates and assumptions concerning                  本集團對未來作出估計及假設。所得之會計
     the future. The resulting accounting estimates will, by               估計如其定義很少相等於相關實際結果。具
     definition, seldom equal the related actual results. The              有重大風險導致下個財政年度之資產及負債
     estimates and assumptions that have a significant risk of             之賬面值作出重大調整之估計及假設討論如
     causing a material adjustment to the carrying amounts of              下：
     assets and liabilities within the next financial year are
     discussed below:


     (a)   Business combinations or assets acquisitions                    (a)   業務合併或資產收購

           Accounting for acquisitions require the Group to                      收購事項之會計處理要求本集團根據
           allocate the cost of acquisition to specific assets                   所收購特定資產及所承擔負債於收購
           acquired and liabilities assumed based on their                       日期之估計公平值將收購成本分配至
           estimated fair values at the date of acquisition. The                 該等資產及負債。本集團已採取程序
           Group has undertaken processes to identify all assets                 識別所有已收購資產及負債。於識別
           and liabilities acquired. Judgements made in                          所有已收購資產、釐定分配至各類所
           identifying all acquired assets, determining the                      收購資產及所承擔負債之估計公平值
           estimated fair value assigned to each class of assets                 以及資產之可使用年期時作出之判斷，
           acquired and liabilities assumed, as well as asset’s                  可能會對其後期間商譽、議價收購以
           useful lives, could materially impact the calculation of              及折舊及攤銷費用之計算造成重大影
           goodwill, bargain purchase and depreciation and                       響。估計公平值乃基於收購日期前後
           amortisation charges in subsequent periods.                           可獲得之資料及管理層認為合理之預
           Estimated fair values are based on information                        期及假設。釐定所收購有形及無形資
           available near the acquisition date and on                            產之估計可使用年期亦須作出判斷。
           expectations and assumptions that have been
           deemed reasonable by management. Determining
           the estimated useful lives of tangible and intangible
           assets acquired also requires judgement.

          The Group has not applied the concentration test to                   本集團並無應用集中度測試以評估所
          assess whether the acquired set of activities and                     收購之一組活動及資產是否並非一項
          assets is not a business. Rather, the Group has                       業務。相反，本集團已根據一組綜合
          assessed a business based on whether an integrated                    活動及資產是否至少包括共同對創造
          set of activities and assets include, at a minimum, an                產出之能力作出重大貢獻之投入及實
          input and a substantive process that together                         質性過程評估業務。倘收購事項不被
          significantly contribute to the ability to create output.             視為業務合併，則不會確認商譽或議
          No goodwill or bargain purchase will be recognised                    價收購。即使交易中存在經濟商譽，
          if the acquisition is not considered as a business                    該金額仍根據其相對公平值分配至所
          combination. Even if there is economic goodwill in                    收購資產。這導致較高之資產基礎，
          the transaction, this amount is allocated to the assets               其後必須進行攤銷或折舊。有關該等
          acquired based on their relative fair values. This                    判斷之不同結論可能對該等投資於本
          results in a higher asset basis that must then be                     集團綜合財務狀況表之呈列及計量方
          amortized or depreciated. Different conclusions                       式造成重大影響。
          around these judgements may materially impact how

          Group.

           area of asset impairment particularly in assessing: (i)               收回之事件；(ii) 可收回金額（即公平值
           whether an event has occurred that may indicate that                  減出售成本與根據於業務中持續使用
           the related asset values may not be recoverable; (ii)                 資產而估計之未來現金流量淨現值之
           whether the carrying value of an asset can be                         較高者）可否支持資產賬面值；及 (iii)
           supported by the recoverable amount, being the                        編製現金流量預測所用之適當主要假
           higher of fair value less costs of disposal and net                   設，包括該等現金流量預測是否以適
           present value of future cash flows which are                          當利率貼現。管理層評估減值時選用
           estimated based upon the continued use of the asset                   之假設（包括現金流量預測所用之貼現
           in the business; and (iii) the appropriate key                        率或增長率假設）之變動可能對減值測
           assumptions to be applied in preparing cash flow                      試所用之淨現值造成重大影響，因而
           projections including whether these cash flow                         影響本集團之財務狀況及經營業績。
           projections are discounted using an appropriate rate.
           Changing the assumptions selected by management
           in assessing impairment, including the discount rates
           or the growth rate assumptions in the cash flow
           projections, could materially affect the net present
           value used in the impairment test and as a result
           affect the Group’s financial condition and results of
           operations.

   Hong Kong Profits Tax is calculated at the rate of 8.25% on         就估計應課稅溢利最多 2 百萬港元而言，香

           and contain a wide range of different terms and                       種不同之條款及條件。除出租人持有
           conditions. The lease agreements do not impose any                    之租賃資產涉及之抵押權益外，租賃

           purposes.

           certain leases across the Group. These are used to                    干租賃。在管理本集團業務所用資產
           maximise operational flexibility in terms of managing                 方面，有關選擇權用以盡量提高業務
           the assets used in the Group’s operations. The                        靈活性。所持大部分延長及終止選擇
           majority of extension and termination options held                    權僅可由本集團行使，且不得由相關
           are exercisable only by the Group and not by the                      出租人行使。
           respective lessor.

   period provided to customers can be longer based on a               戶的關係等多項因素而延長。在接受任何新
   number of factors including the customer’s credit profile           客戶前，本集團評估潛在客戶信貸質素及按
   and relationship with the customers. Before accepting any           客戶界定信貸限額。客戶的限額將會定期審
   new customer, the Group assesses the potential                      閱。本集團設有預期信貸虧損撥備政策，其
   customer’s credit quality and defines credit limits by              乃基於對每名獨立貿易債務人賬目的可回收
   customer. Limits attributed to customers are reviewed               性及賬齡分析作出之評估，及由管理層對每
   regularly. The Group has a policy for allowance for                 名客戶的信譽及過往收款記錄等作出之判斷
   expected credit losses which is based on the evaluation of          而設立。
   the collectability and aging analysis of accounts on every
   individual trade debtor basis and on the management’s

   clients, it normally takes two to three days to settle after        般於該等交易之交易日期後兩至三日內結
